## FET checkpoint retention and minimum-block guard — 2026-08-16 (deployed)

- User-observed behavior: a focused FET run could show a large improvement in
  the live metric (for example `Dạy 1 tiết: 132 → 2`), but pressing Stop then
  discarded that checkpoint because the browser validator required zero total
  violations. This was needlessly strict for a timetable which already carried
  a pre-existing legacy constraint violation before optimisation began.
- `web/pages/phanmon.js` now starts a non-blocking validation snapshot of the
  incumbent before an optimisation run. A terminal candidate or user-stopped
  checkpoint is accepted when it is fully clean, or when every remaining
  violation is the exact same logical violation already present in the
  incumbent. Any new or worsened violation is still rejected and leaves the
  original timetable intact. Automatic first construction remains strict:
  it requires zero candidate violations and never uses this legacy-debt rule.
- `web/pages/tkb-fet-engine.js` now locks the smallest already-valid contiguous
  subject runs needed to satisfy `lessonBlocks.<length>.min` (lengths 2–5)
  before a focused optimiser starts. This prevents a singleton/session/gap
  move from splitting a required block and then presenting an attractive but
  invalid checkpoint. Worker checkpoints now use the engine's retained-best
  snapshot rather than a transient ejection-chain grid, so the displayed
  metric and the timetable applied by Stop identify the same solution.
- The diagnostic panel now distinguishes a candidate-validator failure from
  construction preflight. It no longer describes validator rows as
  `Preflight: 0 hoạt động, 0 slot bị khóa` or represents them as a literal
  zero-slot domain.
- Added regression coverage in
  `e2e_tests/planner_fet_checkpoint_validation_node.test.js` and extended
  `e2e_tests/tkb_fet_engine_node.test.js` for protected block checkpoints.
  Passed verification on 2026-08-16:

  ```powershell
  node --check web/pages/phanmon.js
  node --check web/pages/tkb-fet-engine.js
  node --check web/pages/tkb-fet-worker.js
  node --test --test-concurrency=1 `
    e2e_tests/planner_scheduler_modes_node.test.js `
    e2e_tests/planner_toolbar_layout_node.test.js `
    e2e_tests/fet_client_telemetry_node.test.js `
    e2e_tests/tkb_fet_benchmark_node.test.js `
    e2e_tests/planner_fet_checkpoint_validation_node.test.js
  node --test --test-concurrency=1 --test-name-pattern="FET optimisation locks incumbent minimum lesson blocks|optimizer worker sends only complete improved checkpoints" `
    e2e_tests/tkb_fet_engine_node.test.js
  git diff --check
  ```

  Result: 43 passed, 5 intentionally skipped retired-Hybrid UI checks; the two
  targeted engine regressions passed. The broader historical
  `tkb_fet_engine_node.test.js` suite still targets a newer constraint-compiler
  API that was removed when the user requested the previous fast FET engine be
  restored; it is an existing test/source mismatch and was not hidden or
  modified by this checkpoint fix.
- Production deployment completed through the transactional VPS updater after
  the solver queue drained. The final verification deployment created backup
  `/opt/cherry-scheduler-backups/app-release-20260816-005701.tar.gz`
  (the preceding identical install backup is
  `/opt/cherry-scheduler-backups/app-release-20260816-005332.tar.gz`). The
  updater completed and released its deploy lock; `tkb-app` is active.
- Remote installation and public-origin checks matched the exact local hashes:

  ```text
  42cdd53d3f1e4466419760e068b69620749f3b1ce465429d4811a66ed6e074e9  phanmon.js
  a6b6aab25d6d9200a95c6f42f8c9e3b1d29048f66cc30d3383dbb1d7d8f38b29  tkb-fet-engine.js
  ```

## Security And Repository Notes

- Never place VPS passwords, auth tokens, cookies, or bearer tokens in source,
  logs, diagnostics, or this handoff.
- No certificate with both a private key and the Code Signing EKU was visible in
  the Windows certificate stores during the v1.10 build. When the owner's token
  or certificate is available, sign the final EXE after UPX and add a trusted
  timestamp; never export or copy the private key into this repository.
- The source repository is backed up to the private GitHub repository
  `thoikhoabieucherry/TKBCherry` on branch `main`. Generated packages, databases,
  school workbooks, credentials, and temporary build trees remain ignored.
- The existing `PROJECT.md` and `progress.md` are older planning notes. This file
  is the authoritative current operational handoff.
